- Answer from this content only. If it is not covered, say so; never guess numbers.
- Find relevant pages in index.md by title, aliases (aka) and categories.
- [[X]] links to page X; [[X|y]] and 'y ([[X]])' are text y linking to X. Follow links and each page's 'Linked from' list to gather related facts (e.g. where an item drops or is used).
- Cite the [[Page]] each fact comes from. Reply in the user's language.

# Item Upgrades
Aliases: Item Upgrade
Categories: Pages needing reworking, Article stubs, Temp/Using Skill Aliases
This article needs a rework or a major edit to meet the wiki's standards for an article. You can help the wiki by editing this page or reworking it.
This article was last edited by LunaBot (contribs|logs|block) on 8:06, June 4 2026 (4 months ago)
This article needs more information. Please help expand it.
See more stubs.
Item Upgrades are enchantments, items, or reforges that can boost the stats of Armor and Weapons.
Listed Below is all Possible Upgrades.
## Armor Upgrades
These upgrades can be applied to any armor piece, increasing its stats.
- [[Reforging|Reforges]] range in effects, with the power of the reforge depending on the rarity of the item. Listed below is a small subset of the most popular armor reforges. Reforges cost a few thousand coins to do at higher rarities.
- [[Enchantments]] focus on a variety of effects, some of which effect stats directly (listed below), while others apply non-stat bonuses. The highest level enchantments however are very expensive, and are only recommended for end-game armor (ex: [[Superior Dragon Armor]], [[Perfect Armor]], and the [[Slayer]] [[Armor]] sets). The most helpful stat giving enchantments for end-game armor are: [[Enchantments|Growth VI]], [[Enchantments|Protection VI]], [[Enchantments|True Protection I]], [[Enchantments|Sugar Rush I-III]].
- [[Hot Potato Book|Hot Potato Books]] - similar to high level enchantments, it's recommended to only apply these to high level armor. While these only increase stats by a small amount, each armor piece can have up to 10 of these applied (costing over half a million at shop prices to craft them all) which can provide a significant boost.
- [[Fuming Potato Book|Fuming Potato Books]] - These are Hot Potato Books, but they allow this item to be used 5 times after the 10-book limit. WARNING! If it is under the 10-book limit, it will function as a normal Hot Potato Book.
- [[Star Upgrades]]* can be applied to certain items to gain a 2% stat boost per star outside of Dungeons, and 10% stat boost per star while in Dungeons.
- [[Dungeons|Dungeonizing]]* items will scale its power by the player's [[Catacombs]] level's bonus while in The Catacombs.
- [[Recombobulator 3000]] increases its rarity by 1, amplifying its reforge to the next level.
- [[Gemstone Slot|Gemstone Slots]]* - if available, one [[Gemstone]] can be inserted into each slot. The better the quality of the Gemstone, the better the stats it provides. The effect of a Gemstone is also determined by the rarity of the item, with higher rarities giving higher stats.
- Runes are a cosmetic upgrade that can be applied to items. Runes usually show off particles, and have no real influence on stats.
Note: * indicates that only certain items can have this upgrade.

## Sword Upgrades
These Upgrades can be applied to any sword, But at a cost. Upgrades Like [[Hot Potato Book|Hot Potato Books]] and Higher Level ex: [[Enchantments|Sharpness VI]], [[Enchantments|Ender Slayer VI]], Should only be used on Mid-game weapons or higher, Ex: [[Golem Sword]], [[Aspect of the End]].
- [[Reforging|Reforges]] range in effects, with the power of the reforge depending on the rarity of the item.
- [[Enchantments]] focus on a variety of effects, some of which effect stats directly, while others apply non-stat bonuses.
- [[Hot Potato Book|Hot Potato Books]] - Up to 10 of can be applied to give a weapon +2 [[Damage]] and +2 [[Strength]] per book.
- [[Star Upgrades]]* can be applied to items to give a 2% stat boost per star outside of dungeons, and 10% per star while in dungeons.
- [[Dungeons|Dungeonizing]]* items will scale its power by your Catacombs level's bonus while in dungeons.
- [[Recombobulator 3000]] increases the rarity by 1, amplifying its reforge to the next level.
- [[Gemstone Slot|Gemstone Slots]]* - if available, one [[Gemstone]] can be inserted into each slot. The better the quality of the Gemstone, the better the stats it provides. The effect of a Gemstone is also determined by the rarity of the item, with higher rarities giving higher stats.
Note: * indicates that only certain items can have this upgrade.

# Spawn Villager (NPC)
Categories: Pages needing reworking, Article stubs, Glitches, Non-player characters
This article needs a rework or a major edit to meet the wiki's standards for an article. You can help the wiki by editing this page or reworking it.
This article was last edited by Pigicial's Pet Robot (contribs|logs|block) on 1:10, July 24 2026 (3 months ago)
This article needs more information. Please help expand it.
See more stubs.
Summoned Spawn Villager
Spawn Villagers are NPCs that can be spawned in the [[Dungeon Hub]], using a [[Spawn Egg|Villager Spawn Egg]].
## Spawning
As of now, Spawn Villagers can be summoned by sneaking and right-clicking with the [[Spawn Egg|Villager Spawn Egg]] on the crafting table or an anvil in the Dungeon Hub. Only one can exist per lobby, preventing players from spawning more. Spawn Villager eggs can be obtained from killing mobs using weapons with Jerry Rune applied above the hoppers, that can pick up the Villager Spawn eggs.
## Uses
Spawn Villagers serve no purpose or functionality. They cannot be traded with or talked to.
## Trivia
- Spawn Villagers still rotate their heads while looking at a player, though they cannot be interacted with.
- Spawn Villagers have random professions, if playing on Minecraft Version 1.14 or higher.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Spawn Villagers.|

# UI Items
Aliases: Glitched GUI Items
Categories: Pages needing reworking, More info needed, Items, Glitches, Conjectural articles
This article needs a rework or a major edit to meet the wiki's standards for an article. You can help the wiki by editing this page or reworking it.
This article was last edited by LlamaBeans (contribs|logs|block) on 5:01, July 20 2026 (3 months ago)
User Interface Items are the items obtained from certain GUI Menus using glitches. Many of which turn into their normal item form if transported without a backpack or held in a hand. This includes but is not limited to:
- Anvil
- Crafting Table
- SkyBlock Menu
- Minions
- Trading UI
- Runecrafting Table
- Enchantment Table
- Reforging UI
- Kat
- George

There used to be a bug that allowed players to place it, however, it has been patched, but can still be found on some players' islands.|For UI items: Yes For reverted form: No|Itself|

# Tyashoi Alchemist
Aliases: Alchemy Experiments, Tyashoi
Categories: Pages under review, Confirmations needed, Great Spook Items, Shops, Non-player characters, More info needed, Pages with UIs, Outdated pages
Great Spook Exclusive Tyashoi Alchemist is only available during [[The Great Spook]], when the [[Tyashoi Alchemist]] Tyashoi Alchemist is present in the [[Farm]].
Great Spook 2026
:
Countdown:
Dates and times based on browser time.
[Tyashoi Alchemist]
Type: [[NPC]]
Shop: ✔ Yes
Description: Appears during the Great Spook ([[The Great Spook]]) to sell items.
Location: [[Farm]]
Coordinates: 70.5 71 -85.5
Tyashoi Alchemist is an [[NPC]] located in front of the Alchemy Shop in the [[Farm]] during real-life Halloween. They give the player quests, as long as they have [[Combat|Combat X (10)]] and 10 hours of playtime.

## Gallery
- The Potion Shop while the Tyashoi Alchemist was outside it
- The Tyashoi Alchemist after purchasing Great Spook Artifact The Tyashoi Alchemist after purchasing [[Great Spook Artifact]]
## Trivia
- During the event time in 2021, the appearance of the Alchemy House was changed to a pumpkin while the Tyashoi Alchemist was next to it.
- In 2021, Tyashoi Alchemist only allows one profile to access the quests; in 2022, all profiles can access but every quest can be done once per account.
- Purchasing the [[Great Spook Artifact]] is the only non-cosmetic use of the [[Decorations|Onion]] and [[Decorations|Lettuce]] decorations
- Tyashoi Alchemist is an anagram of He is calamity host, which may or may not be a coincidence.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:

|Dec 5, 2025|Added Tyashoi Alchemist as a Garden Visitor ([[The Garden]]).|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Oct 28, 2021|Added Tyashoi Alchemist, but disabled.|
|Oct 28, 2021|Added functionality for Tyashoi Alchemist.|
|Nov 7, 2021|Disabled Tyashoi Alchemist.|
|Nov 20, 2021|Removed Tyashoi Alchemist.|
|Oct 5, 2022|Re-added the Tyashoi Alchemist with both new and old items.|
|Oct 24, 2023|0.19.6|Re-added the Tyashoi Alchemist with both new and old items.|
|Dec 15, 2025|0.24|Added Tyashoi Alchemist as a Garden Visitor ([[The Garden]]).|
|Oct 14, 2026|Moved the Tyashoi Alchemist from 41, 68, -55 ([[Coordinates]]) to 70.5, 71, -85.5 ([[Coordinates]]), due to the redesign of the [[Hub Island]].|

Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Animates the color of an armor piece between #0B1C5D and #0E1B5C!; This dye also displays a special particle efffect when equipped!; MYTHIC DYE
Black Opal Dye is a [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] Animated [[Dyes|Dye]].
## Obtaining
The Black Opal Dye could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on March 28, 2026. Being [[Rarity|MYTHIC]], it cost 375 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 40,000 purchases.
## Usage
The Black Opal Dye can be applied to a vanilla [[Armor]] piece by combining it in an [[Anvil]] to change its color to animate between ⁠ #0B1C5D and ⁠ #0E1B5C. It also displays a special particle effect when equipped. It costs 100 [[Bits]] to combine the two items.

# Divine Gift/Alpha
Categories: Pages with broken file links, Epic items, Items, Permanent Consumables
[Divine Gift/Alpha]
Divine Gift.png
Divine Gift
Type: Item
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: DIVINE_GIFT
Tooltip: Consumable; Consume this piece of living, breathing RNG to permanently gain +1  Magic Find.; You can consume up to 5 of this item before the way in which it warps probability becomes too much for your tiny brain to handle.; Right-click to consume!; EPIC
A Divine Gift is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Consumables|Permanent Consumable]] item used to increase the player's [[Magic Find]].
## Obtaining
A Divine Gift can be purchased from the [[Regalia Room]].
## Usage
Consuming a Divine Gift grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+2 SkyBlock XP]] and a permanent +1 [[Magic Find]]. Up to 5 Divine Gifts can be consumed in total.
## Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|First Gift|LUCKY! You can feel your odds improving with this item!|
|Second Gift|RNG! You can hear the rare drops calling your name...|
|Third Gift|WOO! The mobs won't have anything left to drop!|
|Fourth Gift|POWER! Every loot table bends to your will...|
|Fifth Gift|DIVINE! With all this Magic Find, perhaps you can find some grass...|
|Attempting to eat more than 5|You've already consumed the maximum amount of this item!|
## Trivia
- "With all this Magic Find, perhaps you can find some grass..." references the running joke that people who spend absurd amounts of time on a game - something SkyBlock players are notorious for doing - need to go outside, or 'touch grass'.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jul 25, 2022|Added Divine Gift I–III ([[Divine Gift]]).|

|Oct 15, 2026|Divine Gift is now a consumable.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Aug 18, 2022|0.14|Added Divine Gift I–III ([[Divine Gift]]).|
|Apr 18, 2023|0.18.3|Added Divine Gift books to the [[Bazaar]].|
|Oct 15, 2024|0.20.7|Fixed Divine Gift having missing information in the Enchantment Guide ([[Enchantment Nerd]]).|

The Empty Hurricane Bottle is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] item.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x [[Empty Storm Bottle]] 320x (5 stacks (320 items total)) [[Orb of Energy]]|Recipe: [[Orb of Energy]] x40 ×8 + [[Empty Storm Bottle]] → Empty Hurricane Bottle|
## Usage
The player can charge an Empty Hurricane Bottle by being struck by lightning and through other methods. Once completely charged up (at 5,000,000 Charges), it becomes a Hurricane in a Bottle.
Much like the [[Enchantments|Expertise]] enchantment, only one Bottle will have charge added to it at a time when multiple are in a player's inventory, starting with the leftmost hotbar slot.
Show/Hide Ways to Charge

|[[Celeste Wand]]|[[Celeste Wand]] Ability|2|0.5s|
|[[Unstable Dragon Armor]]; [[Unstable Dragon Armor]]; [[Unstable Dragon Armor]]; [[Unstable Dragon Armor]]|[[Unstable Dragon Armor]] Full Set Bonus (Strikes once every 15 seconds)|50|0.5s|
|[[Thunder Armor]]; [[Thunder Armor]]; [[Thunder Armor]]; [[Thunder Armor]]|Using [[Thunder Armor]] Tiered Bonus (Strikes when hitting an enemy)|25 (1 Static Charges); 100 (2 Static Charges); 175 (3 Static Charges); 250 (4 Static Charges); 325 (5 Static Charges)|0.5s|
|[[Suspicious Stew]]|[[Suspicious Stew]] Lightning Sound (14.21% chance to occur after eating)|100 (40% Chance); -100 (60% Chance)||

|[[Thunderlord]]|Using [[Thunderlord]] Enchantment (Strikes once every 3 hits)|10|0.5s|
|[[Thunderbolt]]|Using [[Thunderbolt]] Enchantment (Strikes once every 3 hits)|15|0.5s|

||[[Mage Outlaw]] Lightning attack|200|1.5s|

||[[Storm]] Fireball attack|250 (Normal Mode); 375 (Master Mode)|0.5s|

||Wither King ([[The Wither King]]) Lightning attack|500||
### Pulse Ring
Full Thunder Bottles are used to upgrade the rarity of the [[Pulse Ring]]. Total of 5,000,000 Charge is required to max the item, requiring 1 [[Empty Hurricane Bottle|Hurricane in a Bottle]].
## Gallery
The various textures associated with each fill level are shown below.
- Empty Hurricane Bottle.png

- Empty Hurricane Bottle (3).png

- Empty Hurricane Bottle (5).png
- File:Empty Hurricane Bottle (6).png Empty Hurricane Bottle (6).png
- Hurricane in a Bottle.png
## Bugs
- The Empty Hurricane Bottle is currently bugged to not gain charges from certain sources, such as [[Storm]]'s death in [[The Catacombs - Floor VII|F7]] and [[The Catacombs - Floor VII|M7]], therefore it is recommended to use an [[Empty Thunder Bottle]] instead.

The Empty Storm Bottle is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] item.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x [[Empty Thunder Bottle]] 32x [[Orb of Energy]]|Recipe: [[Orb of Energy]] x4 ×8 + [[Empty Thunder Bottle]] → Empty Storm Bottle|
## Usage
The player can charge an Empty Storm Bottle by being struck by lightning and through other methods. Once completely charged up (at 500,000 Charges), it becomes a Storm in a Bottle.
Much like the [[Enchantments|Expertise]] enchantment, only one Bottle will have charge added to it at a time when multiple are in a player's inventory, starting with the leftmost hotbar slot.

### Pulse Ring
Full Thunder Bottles are used to upgrade the rarity of the [[Pulse Ring]]. Total of 5,000,000 Charge is required to max the item, requiring 10 [[Empty Storm Bottle|Storms in a Bottle]].

# Gemstone Crystals
Aliases: Amber Crystal, Amethyst Crystal, Aquamarine Crystal, Citrine Crystal, Gemstone Crystal, Jade Crystal, Jasper Crystal, Jasper Crystal (item), Onyx Crystal, Opal Crystal, Peridot Crystal, Ruby Crystal, Sapphire Crystal, Topaz Crystal
Categories: Pages with broken file links, Crystal Hollows
Gemstone Crystals are collectibles obtained from special tasks within the [[Crystal Hollows]] and also within certain [[Glacite Mineshafts]].
The crystals aren't actual items, but rather achievements that can be tracked in the [[Heart of the Mountain]] menu. To get the rewards from the [[Crystal Nucleus]] the player needs to find and place five crystals: Jade, Amber, Amethyst, Sapphire, and Topaz. Any other crystal, such as Ruby and Jasper, is only used to forge [[Gemstone|Perfect Gemstones]]. All the crystals are obtained in a different way.
The [[Wishing Compass]] can point to the locations of the Crystals or other important related locations.
## [[Crystal Nucleus]] Crystals
### Jade
[[Gemstone Crystals]]
The Jade Crystal is obtained in by finding 4 different [[Scavenged Items]] with a [[Metal Detector]] in the [[Mines of Divan]]. After giving the 4 items to their respective [[Keepers of Divan|Keepers]], the crystal can be taken.
### Amber
[[Gemstone Crystals]]
The Amber Crystal is obtained from the [[Goblin Queen's Den]] in the [[Goblin Holdout]].
In order to obtain it, the player must first get the [[Potions|King's Scent]] [[Potions|Potion Effect]], which is obtained from [[King Yolkar]] after giving him 1 [[Goblin Egg]] of any color.
After obtaining the King's Scent effect, the Goblin Queen's Den can be entered to obtain the crystal. This effect lasts for 20 minutes, and is lost after obtaining the crystal or entering water.
- The King's palace to get the King's Scent effect
- The Goblin Queen's Den, where the Amber Crystal is found.
### Amethyst
[[Gemstone Crystals]]
The Amethyst Crystal is obtained from entering the [[Jungle Temple]] by giving a [[Kalhuiki Door Guardian]] a [[Jungle Key]] (Obtained by killing the [[Key Guardian]] or purchased from [[Odawa]]) and completing the parkour.
- The Jungle Temple where the Amethyst Crystal is guarded by parkour
### Sapphire
[[Gemstone Crystals]]
The Sapphire Crystal is obtained by giving the 6 different [[Professor Robot|Automaton Parts]] (obtained from treasure and loot chests in the [[Precursor Remnants]] or from killing [[Automaton|Automatons]], they cost on [[Bazaar]]) to [[Professor Robot]] in the [[Lost Precursor City]]. After giving the parts to him, he will repair the Automaton Prime, and a door will open which reveals the Crystal.
It is also possible to give a [[Precursor Apparatus]] to Professor Robot to instantly complete the quest.
- The Lost Precursor City with Professor Robot that gives you the Sapphire Crystal
### Topaz
[[Gemstone Crystals]]
The Topaz Crystal is obtained after defeating [[Bal]], located in [[Khazad-dûm]] in the [[Magma Fields]].
After defeating the boss, the Crystal will spawn nearby. Only players who contributed to the boss's elimination may claim it.
## Other Crystals
### Ruby
[[Gemstone Crystals]]
The Ruby Crystal is obtained as a 0.002% drop from mining Ruby or as a reward from the [[Crystal Nucleus]] treasure stash. It has a chance of 0.82441590% to be rolled as a drop from the treasure stash.
It can also be found in a [[Glacite Mineshafts|Glacite Mineshaft]].
### Jasper
[[Gemstone Crystals]]
The Jasper Crystal is a 0.1% drop from killing [[Butterfly|Butterflies]] in the [[Fairy Grotto]]. It can also be obtained by looting chests in the [[Fairy Grotto]], with a 0.1% chance, or as a reward from the [[Crystal Nucleus]] treasure stash. It has a chance of 0.82441590% to be rolled as a drop from the treasure stash.
It can also be found in a [[Glacite Mineshafts|Glacite Mineshaft]].
### Opal, Aquamarine, Onyx, Citrine and Peridot
[[Gemstone Crystals]]
Non-Nucleus Crystals can be found in [[Glacite Mineshafts]]. They have a 25% chance to appear in a mineshaft of a matching type. Only the person who found the mineshaft may claim the crystal. They can also rarely be obtained from [[Frozen Corpses]] that require keys.
## Trivia
- The place where crystals spawn is surrounded by invisible barrier blocks, even if the crystal has not spawned.
- If a player somehow tried to obtain a crystal before they are supposed to (glitching in the [[Jungle Temple]], entering the [[Goblin Queen's Den]] during lag, glitching into Automaton Prime in the [[Lost Precursor City]], etc.), they will receive the following chat message: "You haven't earned this Crystal! Come back when you have! >:-)". To be able to 'earn' a Crystal, one must:
  - Give a [[Jungle Key]] to a [[Kalhuiki Door Guardian]].
  - Give any [[Goblin Egg]] to [[King Yolkar]].
  - Give all Automaton parts, or a [[Precursor Apparatus]], to [[Professor Robot]].
  - Give all [[Scavenged Items]] to the [[Keepers of Divan]].
  - Help kill [[Bal]] in [[Khazad-dûm]].
- All crystals have an unobtainable item form that can be seen in [[The Forge]] when forging [[Gemstone|Perfect Gemstones]].
  - The Jasper Crystal used to drop as an item, and it could be sold on the [[Auction House]]. Jasper Crystals obtained before the change can still be traded and auctioned.
- The Jasper and Ruby Crystals in their item forms can be donated to the [[Museum]].
- Without good luck, the Sapphire Crystal is the hardest one to obtain.
  - [[Mole Pet]] heavily decreases the time it takes to obtain said crystal.

# Hidden Locations
Aliases: Secret Spawn Bunker
Categories: Pages with broken file links, Hidden Locations
Hidden Locations are areas in SkyBlock that are not meant to be accessible and can only be viewed by finding unintended ways in, or by downloading map data and finding where it is hidden.
Please note most of these locations require an [[Aspect of the End]] or glitching through walls by using the [[Horse Pet]] for example.
This page is just meant for fun, and this knowledge serves no practical purpose.
Some entries below describe areas that no longer exist because the map was rebuilt. Those are kept for the record and are marked as removed where known.
## Windmill Roof
Location: [[Coordinates|70, 76, -148]] You used to be able to get inside the roof of the windmill at the Farm with the [[Aspect of the End]]. You could stand inside the windmill and look up at the mossy stone brick, jump and use the [[Aspect of the End]]'s ability twice.
[AHN] (This item or feature is currently only available on the Alpha Hypixel Network.)
This no longer exists because of the new map.
## Catacombs Entrance Cage
Location: [[Coordinates|-67, 61, -136]]
You can get inside the cage at Catacombs Entrance with an [[Ender Pearl]].
Go away from the cage so you can throw an ender pearl into the cage (Pearls go through iron bars and glass).
Another way of getting into the cage is to sprint towards the cage and use an [[Aspect of the End]] to teleport into the catacombs entrance cage. This may take a few attempts.
## Secret Spawn Bunker
Location: [[Coordinates|9, 65, -100]]
The Secret Spawn Bunker is a small room at spawn with 2 torches, logs in 3 (or 5, depending on how you count) corners, and a staircase leading to nowhere.
## Jungle Island Parkour
Location: [[Coordinates|-404, 85, -72]]
An unreleased parkour course. Much of the outside of the course is easily accessible (starting at [[Coordinates|-446, 99, -93]]). There was a wall at [[Coordinates|-443, 99, -90]] that prevented further entry into the hidden internal part of the course, but it has been removed. It is actually possible to see the hidden area by looking down through the ice at [[Coordinates|-405, 117, -73]]. It is also possible to glitch through the stone wall using an [[Aspect of the End]] or a horse pet (which is easier). At the end of the course is a slime launcher which doesn't actually work yet. It leads to the surface but is currently closed off.
At some unknown time, this area became completely inaccessible. The ice that would normally be at the bottom of the lake and the space below it has been filled with proper blocks. The outer parkour area that would normally lead to the closed off entry is also removed save for one single platform at the start. More information is required on the possibility of the entire area being filled with blocks or not.
## Stone Pillar Hallway
Location: [[Coordinates|2, 70, 50]]
A large hallway with very tall stone pillars with sea lanterns attached to them. Nothing seems to naturally connect to this hallway, or hint at its use. It's located inside the hub [[Mountain]], with one end near the [[Wizard Tower]] / over the right-wing of the High-Level Mountain area (see above), and the other end near the [[Carrot]] farm in the base of the Mountain (which is near the [[Library]]).
With the Hub revamp, while this structure still exists, it has been filled in with stone blocks replacing all air blocks. However, the andesite pillars remain intact.
## Wizard Tower Roof
You can get inside the Roof of the Wizard Tower using the [[Aspect of the End]]/[[Aspect of the Void|Void]]'s Etherwarp Transmission ability. There is an odd stone pillar hanging from the roof.
## Community Center 3rd Floor
You can find there's an extra floor in the [[Community Center]]. Using [[Aspect of the End]] you can teleport inside. First, start breaking the glass at [[Coordinates|-3, 77, -116]]. Then quickly teleport inside with your [[Aspect of the End|AOTE]].
Or you can just teleport inside using an ender pearl. (Same as in Catacombs Entrance Cage)
This no longer exists due to the new map.
## Community Center Basement
Location: [[Coordinates|-30, 53, -106]] There are two parts in the Community Center's basement. You can glitch into the first part by using the Horse pet glitch on the rightmost wall panel on the back, at [[Coordinates|-1, 68, -118]]. In the first part, you can glitch into the second part at [[Coordinates|1, 68, -115]]. In the second part, you can glitch into the first part at [[Coordinates|2, 68, -114]].
Rats can be found here when 56ms is a Minister. It is unknown whether this existed before the Village was destroyed by [[Dante]].
## Sponge Room
Location: In the basement of the [[Hexatorum]] (formerly dubbed the Guild House), at the east south corner.
You can find this small space with sponges by using rock pet and horse pet glitch at east south corner of the [[Hexatorum]]'s (formerly dubbed the Guild House) basement.
However, likely due to the introduction of the [[Shen's Auction]] area sitting close to it, the space has been covered up, but small parts of the sponge still remain and can still be seen if the blocks at the corner are broken.
File:Hexatorum-secret-sponge.png
## Outside of The Dungeon Hub
It is possible to access this location by using the Ether Transmission ability on the [[Wool|Gray Wool]] on the ceiling of the Dungeon Hub.
It was previously possible to access this location by spawning a [[Horse Pet]] in the dungeons hubs door, which is right behind [[Mort]]. The glitch however is patched and the Dungeon Hub uses mining fatigue to prevent breaking blocks.
## Ceiling of the Dwarven Mines
In previous versions of the Dwarven Mines before the Crystal Hollows update, there existed a hole starting from [[Coordinates|66, 222, -83]] that would lead straight up to the ceiling of the [[Dwarven Mines]]. Players would use the [[Aspect of the End]] to climb up the hole but must use other means to push themselves up the ceiling (using the [[Grappling Hook]] or [[Tarantula Boots]]) as the build limit prevented the use of teleportation abilities. It is mostly bare up there with the exception of the secret text that says "WHY ARE YOU UP HERE?", "COS", and "THE NERVE!". It was also a useful spot to mine cobblestone at the time with how expansive it was. The hole is now blocked off with the release of the Crystal Hollows update.
Other methods getting up there involving the Horse pet are also patched out.
A video showing the message can be seen here.
## Dark Auction
You get in the [[Dark Auction]] area in the hub by glitching through the wall using ghost blocks and [[Bonzo's Staff]], followed by another ghost block exploit to get around the iron door at the basement. This area is commonly used by high-level players to grind [[Headless Horseman|Headless Horsemen]] as the area is blocked off, preventing it from being pushed into unfavorable spots or being swarmed by too many players at once.
While attending the Dark Auction itself, it was previously possible to walk out the basement and the shack using a Horse pet. However, pets and many abilities have since been disabled in Dark Auction lobbies, and custom mining mechanics are put in place to prevent ghost block exploits.
Inside the Dark Auction House The Dark Auction basement
## Cow Lab
In the [[Glowing Mushroom Cave]] after the Moby Chumming update, a secret area can be found starting from blocked off ladders at [[Coordinates|175, 43, -469]] under the dirt. Using ghost block exploits, it is possible to climb down these ladders and see what appears to be a secret lab of sorts with two upside-down cows (tagged as Dinnerbone), both of which can be killed and appear to act as normal cows otherwise. It is unknown what this area is supposed to be considering the update it appeared in.
This has now become [[Underground Lab|Jake's Lab]] and is used in various stages of Beth's quest.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Unknown Date|Added multiple Hidden Locations.|

Jerry Dye is a [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] Animated [[Dyes|Dye]] that could be purchased from a [[Fire Sale]].
## Obtaining
The Jerry Dye could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on April 1, 2026. Being [[Rarity|MYTHIC]], it cost 375 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 16,767 purchases.
## Usage
The Jerry Dye can be applied to a vanilla [[Armor]] piece by combining it in an [[Anvil]] to change its color to animate between ⁠ #6F5753 and ⁠ #715854. It also displays a special particle effect when equipped. It costs 100 [[Bits]] to combine the two items.

# Kuudra
Aliases: Basic Kuudra, Burning Kuudra, Fiery Kuudra, Hot Kuudra, Infernal Kuudra, Kudra, Kuudra Collection, Kuudra Knockers, Kuudra Mobs, Kuudra Shop, Kuudra collection, Kuudra shop, Perk Shop, Perk shop, Routes
Categories: Pages with broken file links, Mobs, Mobs without Bestiary data, Mob pages missing internal IDs, More info needed, Outdated pages, Pages with UIs, Crimson Isle, Kuudra
Overgrown lava Kraken. The more sulphur it consumes the stronger it becomes.
— [[Crag]]
[Kuudra]
Type: [[Mob]]
Spawn Location: [[Kuudra's Hollow]]
Mob Type: Infernal ([[Infernal (Mob Type)]]) Cubic ([[Cubic (Mob Type)]])
Kuudra is a [[Bosses|BOSS]] found in the [[Kuudra's Hollow]], accessed from the [[Forgotten Skull]]. [[Kuudra Keys]] are used to unlock Reward Chests unlocked after the fight.
## Tiers
There are 5 tiers of the Kuudra boss fight: Basic, Hot, Burning, Fiery, and Infernal. Each of them have differing [[Factions|Faction]] [[Factions|Reputation]] requirements and mob wave spawn timings.
|Tier|Requirements|SkyBlock XP|Seconds; Per Wave|
|-|-|-|-|
|Basic|Have [[Elle]] take you to Kuudra|20 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|35s|
|Hot|Complete the main quest 1000 Reputation ([[Factions]]) in any faction Beat the previous tier|40 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|30s|
|Burning|3000 Reputation ([[Factions]]) in any faction Beat the previous tier|60 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|25s|
|Fiery|7000 Reputation ([[Factions]]) in any faction Beat the previous tier|80 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|20s|
|Infernal|12000 Reputation ([[Factions]]) in any faction Beat the previous tier|100 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|15s|
## Boss Fight
Kuudra's boss fight consists of multiple phases, depending on the selected tier.
### Phase 1: Crates
Phase One involves using a Lava Rod to fish up Supply Crates in the lava surrounding the main platform and bringing them to the center of the arena. Players can either use a [[Starter Lava Rod]], [[Topaz Rod]], [[Magma Rod]], [[Inferno Rod]], or [[Hellfire Rod]] to fish up the crates; those lacking a Lava Rod can purchase an Elle's Lava Rod from the Perk Shop.
Once the supply crate reaches the platform, players must stand still until the meter reaches 100% to pick up the crate. To reduce the time taken to fish and pick up supply crates, one can upgrade the Steady Hands perk unlocked from the Specialist route and have a [[Kuudra's Kidney]], [[Kuudra's Lung]], or [[Kuudra's Heart]] in their inventory or [[Accessory Bag]].
Taking too long to place the supply crate or taking too much damage will cause the crate to fall back into the lava.
### Phase 2: Ballista
Phase Two involves building the Ballista by using the supplies obtained during Phase One. By sneaking and left-clicking the piles of supplies on the ground, players make progress towards completing the construction of the Ballista, which occurs once the meter reaches 100%. The Ballista will be the primary weapon that players use against Kuudra.
To reduce the time taken to build the Ballista, one can upgrade the Ballista Mechanic perk unlocked from the Specialist route or have a [[Kuudra's Kidney]], [[Kuudra's Lung]], or [[Kuudra's Heart]] in their inventory or [[Accessory Bag]].
During this phase, mobs will attempt to attack the Ballista, bringing the meter back down to 0%.
### Phase 3: Fuel
Phase Three involves fishing up Fuel Cells to fuel the Ballista constructed during Phase Two. They can be fished up with a Lava Rod, similar to supply crates, and are used with the Ballista to attack Kuudra. Once all 4 Fuel Cells have been secured, players can mount the Ballista and fire at Kuudra.
To reduce the time taken to fish up Fuel Cells, one can upgrade the Steady Hands perk unlocked from the Specialist route.
During Burning Tier Kuudra and above, Kuudra must be stunned before the Ballista can be fired: otherwise, very little damage will be dealt and Kuudra will submerge.
### Phase 4: Stomach
Kuudra's stomach
Phase Four is exclusive to Burning Tier and higher, and involves players firing themselves into Kuudra's stomach. In order to enter Kuudra's stomach, players can either:
- Purchase a Human Cannonball, allowing you to fire yourself into Kuudra's mouth;
- Stand in the Consumption Zone, allowing yourself to be consumed by Kuudra's tentacles.
Several Orbs can be found in Kuudra's stomach, which must be broken to stun Kuudra. Breaking the orbs with a Pickaxe will reduce their health by 5%; players who do not have a Pickaxe can purchase a Elle's Pickaxe in the Kuudra Perk Shop.
During this time, several [[Magma Bacteria]] will spawn, who fire projectiles at the player that deal knockback. When Kuudra submerges, the stomach will fill up with lava and all players within will be launched out.
### Phase 5: Lair
Kuudra's true lair
Phase Five is exclusive to the Infernal Tier that begins once Kuudra's health reaches 25%. It involves players dodging Kuudra's tentacles and firing at Kuudra's head until its health reaches 0.
The main arena of [[Kuudra's Hollow]] collapses to reveal a lower section surrounded by lava, with [[Wandering Blaze|Wandering Blazes]] surrounding the top of the arena, killing any players above them.
During this phase, all deaths are considered Final Kills: however, players can revive if their party has a member alive who has the Support Route selected, who can revive players using [[Tokens]].
## Kuudra Mobs
A multitude of different mobs appear during the Kuudra fight. If too many mobs are still alive, then mobs still stop spawning.

## Loot
Each tier of Kuudra has its own loot pool that its Reward Chests roll from. There will be a free chest, which rewards [[Crimson Essence]], and a paid chest: depending on the tier selected, you will need its respective [[Kuudra Keys|Kuudra Key]] (e.g. an [[Kuudra Keys|Infernal Kuudra Key]] is required to unlock the paid chest in Kuudra Infernal Tier).
You are guaranteed to obtain [[Crimson Essence]], [[Kuudra Teeth]], an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], or an [[Enchanted Book]], as well as an extra piece of gear. Having the [[Kuudra Teeth Plaque]] maxed out at [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] grants a small chance to obtain a [[Kuudra Mandible]].

## Collection
Kuudra also has a [[Collections|Boss Collection]]. Defeating Kuudra will increment progress towards the Kuudra Collections, which primarily rewards the [[Kuudra Pet]] and [[Kuudra Chunk]] upgrades, as well as [[Crimson Essence]] and [[Kuudra Teeth]]. The amount of progress is directly related to the tier completed; for example, a Basic tier will only grant 1 point, while an Infernal tier will grant 5 points.
|LVL|Required|Reward|XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|I|10|Common Kuudra Pet ([[Kuudra Pet]]) Reward; 400 (6 stacks plus 16) Crimson ([[Crimson Essence]]) Essence; [[Kuudra Teeth]] Reward|+10|
|II|100|[[Kuudra Chunk]] Reward; 1,000 (15 stacks plus 40) Crimson ([[Crimson Essence]]) Essence; 2 [[Kuudra Teeth]] Reward|+15|
|III|500|Meaty Kuudra Chunk ([[Kuudra Chunk]]) Reward; 2,500 Crimson ([[Crimson Essence]]) Essence; 4 [[Kuudra Teeth]] Reward|+20|
|IV|2,000|Hardened Kuudra Chunk ([[Kuudra Chunk]]) Reward; 10,000 Crimson ([[Crimson Essence]]) Essence; 8 [[Kuudra Teeth]] Reward|+25|
|V|5,000|Enriched Kuudra Chunk ([[Kuudra Chunk]]) Reward; 20,000 (312 stacks plus 32) Crimson ([[Crimson Essence]]) Essence; 12 [[Kuudra Teeth]] Reward|+30|
## Tips
- For instant orb breaking, it is recommended to have Pickobulus selected in the [[Heart of the Mountain]].

[Portal Dye]
Type: Dye ([[Dyes]])
Rarity: MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: DYE_PORTAL
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Animates the color of an armor piece between #5909C1 and #D412F0!; This dye also displays a special particle efffect when equipped!; MYTHIC DYE
Portal Dye is a [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] Animated [[Dyes|Dye]].
## Obtaining
The Portal Dye could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on July 13, 2024. Being [[Rarity|MYTHIC]], it cost 375 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 60,000 purchases.
## Usage
The Portal Dye can be applied to a vanilla [[Armor]] piece by combining it in an [[Anvil]] to change its color to animate between ⁠ #5909C1 and ⁠ #D412F0. It also displays a special particle effect when equipped. It costs 100 [[Bits]] to combine the two items.
#5909c1 → #560aba → #520cb1 → #4d0da7 → #490f9c → #440f92 → #3f0f8a → #3c1081 → #38107b → #361076 → #371078 → #390f7c → #3a1080 → #3c0f85 → #3e0e8c → #400d92 → #430b9a → #450aa1 → #4909a8 → #4c08af → #5107b5 → #5608bc → #5c0ac1 → #620dc5 → #680fc9 → #6f12ce → #7814d1 → #8117d5 → #8a18d9 → #9419dc → #9e1adf → #a71ae3 → #b01ae5 → #b919e7 → #c117ea → #c816ec → #cf14ef → #d412f0
### Trivia
- On the [[Alpha Hypixel Network]], it was possible for a short time to donate Portal Dye to [[Vincent]] in order to get [[Bucket of Dye]] with a unique and now unobtainable sprite.
### Gallery
- Portal Dyed Leather Armor Portal Dyed Leather Armor
- Portal Dyed Leather Armor (Static)
- Bucket of Dye dyed with Portal Dye during Alpha Hypixel Network testing Bucket of Dye dyed with Portal Dye during [[Alpha Hypixel Network]] testing

|Jul 13, 2024|Started the [[Fire Sale]] for the Portal Dye.|

# SkyBlock 1.0
Categories: Pages with broken file links
SkyBlock 1.0 is an upcoming major update to SkyBlock. It was set to fully release in 2022, then delayed to 2024 or 2025, and will probably release sometime in the late 2020s. Certain parts of 1.0 will be released along the way to the full release. SkyBlock will leave the Prototype Lobby once 1.0 is fully released.
The update will contain a large amount of content, such as new islands and features. In addition, many existing areas of the game will receive a revamp and rebalance using the experience with those areas so far.
The development of 1.0 is assisted by design threads posted on the Hypixel Forums. In each design thread, an early design draft for changes to a certain area is shown, and the community can give feedback through a linked survey or in the thread itself. Nothing within the design threads is final; all mentioned additions and changes are subject to large changes.
- Main thread
  - Admin response 1
- Thread 1 - Skills
  - Admin response 1
  - Admin response 2
- Thread 2 - Private Islands and SkyBlock Levels
  - Admin response 1
- Thread 3 - Minions and Collections
  - Admin response 1
- Thread 4 - Playing alone and together
  - Admin response 1
  - Admin response 2
- Thread 5 - Few smaller ideas
  - Admin response 1
  - Admin response 2
- Thread 6 - Gear and Modifiers
  - Admin response 1
- Thread 7 - Economy
- Thread 8 - RNG and Combat
- Thread 9 - Foraging Island and Minecraft version
## Additions
### Locations
[[Museum]]:
- Items can be donated to the [[Curator]] to be put on display
- The player's can be appraised by [[Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth III]]
- Donated items become Co-op Soulbound and cannot be traded with player's outside of your [[Co-op]] afterwards
- Players receive rewards according to the number of unique items they have donated
### Items
Armor:
- 4 new Armor types: Cloaks, Gauntlets (gloves), Belts, and Necklaces
- The best versions of each new piece will be dropped from specific content
  - Example: The [[Colosseum]] will drop Belts
Dyes:
- Can be applied to any armor which is Leather in order to change their color
  - All dyed armor will have an icon in their descriptions, signifying that they are dyed
    - This will not affect existing dyed "Exotic" armor pieces
- Drop extremely rarely from certain mobs
  - Example: [[Wolf|Wolves]] in the Ruins will extremely rarely drop ⁠ #B2FFFF Celeste Dye
Group buff items:
- More items which benefit all nearby players will be added
## Changes
Dungeons:
- The total % of secrets and speed required to max their scores depends on the floor
|Floor|% of secrets required|Maximum time|
|-|-|-|
|F1|30%|10:00|
|F2|40%|10:00|
|F3|50%|10:00|
|F4|60%|12:00|
|F5|70%|10:00|
|F6|85%|12:00|
|F7|100%|12:00|
|M1-M6|100%|8:00|
- Every 1% of secrets found above the cap will grant +0.5% Catacombs EXP
- The first completion XP bonus will instead be given on the first 5 completions
- The first 5 successful dungeon runs of a day will give 40% extra Catacombs EXP
[[Accessory Bag]]:
- Applying a reforge to the Accessory Bag permanently unlocks it for freely swapping reforges
- Reforges will only be able to be applied directly to the Bag
- New system for Accessory reforges:
  - All accessories' rarities will be summed up as Reforge Points
  - The power of the reforge scales with the number of Reforge Points
- Accessory Bag grants some stats free to spend based on the number of owned Accessories
Armor sets:
- Most full set bonuses will be changed to scale with the number of worn pieces
  - Example: [[Wise Dragon Armor]]
    - 2 pieces - 8% [[Mana]] cost reduction
    - 3 pieces - 18% [[Mana]] cost reduction
    - 4 pieces - 33% [[Mana]] cost reduction
Quest items:
- Items such as the File:Initiate Campfire Badge.png Campfire Badge will become Co-op Soulbound
Loot sharing:
- Will be expanded to other areas of the game
[[Essence]]:
- New types will be added
- Essence can be used to upgrade almost all gear
- The effects of stars will take effect globally
  - Effects of stars will be visible in item tooltips
Rusty:
- [[Rusty]] will become a 'Lost and Found' NPC where players can buy back quest/npc items
  - This is so that players can gain back items that has been lost and cannot be obtained again, such as the [[Campfire Badge]]
Garden:
- Location accessible from private islands starting at [[Farming|Farming X (10)]]
- Will contain new types of crops and farming mechanics, methods, and patterns
Modifier system:
- Streamlines the item upgrading process
- Allows players to apply all owned modifiers to an item, from anywhere in their storage
- Can instantly craft and purchase missing modifiers
SkyBlock Levels:
- Gauge of a player's progress through the game compared to others
- An enhancement of the already existing Skill Average and Weight systems, making it inclusive of all areas of SkyBlock and giving it its own learning curve and progression
- Leveled up by completing certain tasks
  - Example values:
    - Leveling up a Skill: 25 EXP
    - Finishing a Quest: 5 to 25 EXP
    - Bestiary Milestone: 3 EXP
    - Beating a specific Slayer boss for the first time: 5 to 10 EXP
    - Slayer Levels: 10/15 EXP
    - [[Museum]] milestones: 2-15 EXP
    - Every other instance of a significant milestone in the game would give an amount of EXP
    - Infinite sources of EXP will be avoided in order to prevent balancing issues for top players
- Rewards perks such as Private Island gameplay elements
- Unlocks parts of a global quest line
Telekinesis:
- Will be changed from an enchantment to a global perk unlocked at SkyBlock level 5
## Planned additions
This list is incomplete. You can help by expanding it.
All features are not final and are subject to large changes.
### Gameplay
Island matchmaking:
- Island system will try to put players of similar strength together
  - Only 2 different lobbies - one for early-mid game players, and another for late-end game players
  - Will not stop players from being warped into the other lobby types
Party Finder:
- Matchmaking similar to modern multiplayer game matchmaking
  - Automatically matches queued players according to their level, class and floor
Quests:
- Addition of a global quest line with side quests, unlocked with SkyBlock levels
Skill trees:
- May be added to skills apart from [[Mining]]
- Leveling up Skills such as [[Combat]] could reward points to spend within the skill trees
### General
Lore:
- Will be expanded upon in the quest line
## Planned changes
This list is incomplete. You can help by expanding it.
All features are not final and are subject to large changes.
### Items
Collection items:
- Unused items within Collections, such as [[Cactus Armor]], will be tweaked to be integrated into their relevant Skill's progression
Conversion into [[Dungeon Items]]:
- Will be removed for all non-dungeon items
  - This change may not take effect
Keystone Accessories:
- Will be more powerful and have more impactful effects
- Only a few can be used at a time
- May not release until a while after 1.0
Rarity:
- Many items will have their rarity reduced by 1
  - In order to "better represent the spread of items in the current state of the game"
Requirements:
- Will be added to most Skill-related items
[[Training Dummy]]:
- Will be able to show total DPS
- Will be able to simulate any mob from the [[Bestiary]]
- May be able to simulate dungeon stats
- Additional enhancements
### Mobs
All mobs:
- Will "have its own uniqueness, both in what they do and in what they drop"
Mob levels:
- Will be assigned using an algorithm rather than arbitrarily
[[Sea Creatures]]:
- Will drop important and rare [[Alchemy]] ingredients
### Locations
[[Colosseum]]:
- Will receive a revamp with PvE content
- Will reward Belts
Private Island:
- Adding rare events and special spawns to the Private Island
- SkyBlock level progress rewards options for customizing private island gameplay
  - New mobs spawning on the island as SkyBlock levels increase, which will have useful drops
  - Certain levels unlock automation options such as sorting systems
  - SkyBlock level 15: Rare NPC can spawn on the island
  - SkyBlock level 20: Rare dragons fly across the island, can be shot for a reward
- All potentially dangerous content will be toggleable
### Gameplay
[[Co-op]] system:
- Will be changed such that co-op members will have less opportunity to ruin other members
- Parity with solo profiles such that there are no advantages to either system
Collection/Recipe:
- Collection have relevant recipes that sink a large amount of that resource
- Recipes unlocked from collections will be slowly earned (slower learning curve)
- Late game items' recipe includes late game drops and have a high requirement
- Unused items from early game will have a purpose so late game players will still use these items
- Balance Collections and making them fair will reward players
- Collecting resources by hand will not be in competition with minions (WIP IDEA)
  - For example, Collecting resources by hand might drop a special item that can be used in specific crafts that minions cannot get
[[Dungeons]]:
- Fix Party Warping after a dungeon has finished
- Boss collection milestones will be implemented to the rest of the game
Fairy Souls:
- [[Fairy Souls]] will not reward stats anymore, but instead reward other things such as Storage Space and SkyBlock EXP
  - Losing the stats will not make the game harder as the players can get these stats back somewhere else
Quests:
- System similar to [[Commissions]] for [[Mining]] may be added for other skills
- Commissions could be introduced earlier in [[Mining]] progression
- Global system will receive improvements in order to implement better quest content
- The global quest line will finally unveil the story of the world of SkyBlock and will lead players to a familiar place.
[[Skills]]:
- Will have improved integration with global progression
- Will have improved progression within the Skill itself
  - Progression within a skill will involve item progression and changes to the gameplay style
  - Prevents monotonous progression in skills such as [[Farming]], where it is only effective to do a single farming task from level 0 to 60
  - Prevents skills from adversely affecting other skills' progression
- Tweaks to Skill leveling bonuses to make them more rewarding
  - Coin rewards are currently "mediocre"
- Cap most Skill rewards at Skill level 50. Any progress past 50 would be mostly cosmetic
[[Slayer]] ramp-up:
- Each Slayer boss kill makes the subsequent boss stronger, but increases the rewards
- Resets every SkyBlock year
- High score will be saved each year
### General
Economy:
- Balancing rewards and money making capabilities of all skills with each other
- NPC selling system like a market. Depending on the influx of money, items sold to NPC will either drop or rise, hitting a cap at some point
Elite 500:
- Group of 500 players with the highest SkyBlock level
- Will have custom Tab menu tag
[[Enchantments]]:
- Many more enchantments will be added
  - More defensive, support and "technical" enchantments
  - More offensive enchantment options
- Each item will have a set amount of enchantments it can hold
  - Each item may be able to hold different amounts of offensive and defensive enchantments etc.
  - Allows adding an indefinite amount of enchantments without increasing players' power levels
- Balancing to enchantments, especially buffs to weaker ones
- [[One For All]] will receive "changes, including nerfs"
[[Healing]]:
- May be buffed to fit the increased [[Health]] of players if necessary
[[Minions]]:
- Additional options around Minions, such as Minion placement and new Minion types
- Special Minion type at SkyBlock level 22, which performs a different task than generating resources
- Make Minions generate less Skill EXP
- Minions will be constantly balanced so every minion will have some kind of use
- Automatic Shipping slots might be reworked to a third minion upgrade slot that only unlocks after the minion hits tier 10
- Fuels and Minion speed can be nerfed as they are generating resources too fast
- Minion upgrades, especially fuel-type minion upgrades and diamond spreading, are wrong and these upgrades make players choose minion speed over minion resource
- Minions will have 3 different perks:
  - Minion Personalities (provide small strategic decisions to maximise the minion's usefulness)
  - Special Minions (Unlocked through SkyBlock Levels and don't generate resources but can facilitate gameplay or to optimize your setup)
  - Minion Levels (Every time a player collects the minion, the minion will level up and gain some perks, including speed and storage)
Progression:
- Make goals clearer to players for each stage of the game
- Make mobs have their own personality (what they do) and drop different items
- Balance gear and PvE
Salvage:
- Non-dungeon gear will be salvageable for essence
- New confirmation menu when salvaging important or expensive items
Stats:
- Armor and reforges will grant more [[Health]] and less [[Defense]]
Telekinesis:
- Will be changed from an enchantment to a global perk unlocked at SkyBlock level 5
[[Wardrobe]]:
- Will be changed to function with the 4 new armor pieces
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Sep 22, 2021|SkyBlock 1.0 was first discussed.|
Linked from: [[Changelog]]

Spooky Dye is a [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] Animated [[Dyes|Dye]].
## Obtaining
Spooky Dye was obtainable from [[Taylor]] in the Fall 2025 Bundle. It could be purchased for 375 SkyBlock Gems.
## Usage
Spooky Dye can be applied to any vanilla [[Armor]] piece by combining the two items in an [[Anvil]] to animate the hex color of that piece between ⁠ #5118A5 and ⁠ #5219A8. It costs 100 [[Bits]] to combine the two items.

Warden Dye is a [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] Animated [[Dyes|Dye]].
## Obtaining
The Warden Dye could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on January 27, 2024. Being [[Rarity|MYTHIC]], it cost 375 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 40,000 purchases.
## Usage
The Warden Dye can be applied to a vanilla [[Armor]] piece by combining it in an [[Anvil]] to change its color to animate between ⁠ #034150 and ⁠ #009295. It costs 100 [[Bits]] to combine the two items.

# Dead Man's Hunt
Aliases: DEAD MANS JOURNAL, Dead Man, Dead Man's, Dead Man's Journal, Dead Mans Hunt, Deadman
Categories: Pages with content under updates, Confirmations needed, More info needed, Pages with UIs, Aura's Mayorship
This page contains in-game content about an ongoing event. Due to the nature of this page, latest discoveries are likely to be missing. You can help by contributing relevant information.
Dead Man's Hunt
Currently ongoing community event!
Riddles found: 5⧸14
The Dead Man's Hunt is an ongoing hunt for a currently unknown reward.
## Description
The Dead Man's Hunt may be completed by solving riddles in the [[Dead Man's Hunt|Dead Man's Journal]] to fill it out.[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]
### Items
|Step|Slot|Item|Riddle|Method|First Obtained|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|0|[[Dead Man's Chestplate]]|[[Dead Man's Chestplate]]||Drop from [[Frozen Corpses]]; 1.506% (1 in 66.4 rolls) from Umber Corpses ([[Frozen Corpses]]) and Tungsten Corpses ([[Frozen Corpses]]); [More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; When this info is added, remove this.) from Vanguard Corpses ([[Frozen Corpses]])|October 6, 2026|
|1|[[Dead Man's Hunt]]|Dead Man's Journal ([[Dead Man's Hunt]])|"That horribly rigid, overfrozen water in nearby lake almost kills everything."|Obtained by throwing [[Dead Man's Chestplate]] into the [[Great Glacite Lake]]|October 6, 2026|
### Riddles
After obtaining the [[Dead Man's Hunt|Dead Man's Journal]], completing riddles unlock new slots inside of it:
The riddles are collapsed by default to prevent spoilers.:
|Step|Riddle|Method|First Obtained|
|-|-|-|-|
|2|To find which comes next, look to the heart of still waters. Only what is winter, pressed beyond winter, may wake it.|Obtained by throwing [[Enchanted Packed Ice]] into exact center of [[Great Glacite Lake]] (approx. 4, 132, 388 ([[Coordinates]]) - 4, 132, 391 ([[Coordinates]]))|October 6, 2026|
|3|To find which comes next, drift, not land. Let it fall from afar.|Obtained by throwing [[Feather Talisman]] into the [[Great Glacite Lake]] and being 50+ blocks away when it hits the water|October 7, 2026|
|4|That which comes next does not heed the first call, nor the second.|Obtained by throwing [[Arachne's Calling]] into the [[Great Glacite Lake]] 3 times within 10s|October 7, 2026|
|5|To find what comes next, look to past offerings that still linger.|Obtained by throwing a [[Griffin Feather]] into the [[Great Glacite Lake]] while 3 [[Arachne's Calling]] are in the water|October 7, 2026|
|6|To find out which comes next, the body is not enough. Assemble that which remains when it is gone.|||
## Game Messages
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Obtaining Dead Man's Journal ([[Dead Man's Hunt]])|The lake consumes the Dead Man's Chestplate, but remains ravenous. ... Something floats to the surface. You reach out and took the Dead Man's Journal that had been hidden inside the chestplate.|
|Completing a riddle|Your Dead Man's Journal updated itself!|
## Trivia
- The Dead Man's Hunt was added by 7[[Ranks|[YOUTUBE]]] Dra2oon as part of the [[Minister Election|Minister Elections]].
  - He had no design impact on the hunt.
- The Dead Man's Journal may be related to [[Divan (disambiguation)|Divan]].
  - Divan is the first king of Dwarves, and the one who discovered [[Crystal Hollows]], [[Divan Fragment]], and [[Divan's Alloy]]. His status is currently unknown, though most likely deceased.
- The community heavily collaborates on solving the hunt's riddles via the Official Hunters Discord.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Oct 6, 2026|0.27.2|Added Dead Man's Hunt.|
Linked from: [[Changelog]], [[Dead Man's Chestplate]], [[Great Glacite Lake]], [[Hunt (disambiguation)]], [[Hypixel SkyBlock Wiki]], [[Hypixel SkyBlock Wiki/sandbox]], [[Minister Election]], [[Rusty]], [[Rusty/List]]

# Sacks
Aliases: Sack, Sax
Categories: Pages with content under updates, Sacks
This page contains in-game content which has been recently added or is being updated. Content on this page might be missing, out of date, inaccurate or incorrect. You can help by contributing relevant information.
[Sacks]
Sacks allow the player to store a certain amounts of different types of items while held in the inventory or a [[Sack of Sacks]]. Players can collect the items stored in the sack by opening it and then clicking the item that they want to collect, or using the /gfs command. Enchanted sacks function the same as regular sacks; they just store the enchanted form of items. Enchanted sacks only have the large tier, and work along the [[Personal Compactor 4000]] and its higher tiers.
Sacks do not directly store items, but rather having one in your inventory or [[Sack of Sacks]] grants you additional storage space where items can be stored. If sacks are put away in a chest, the items remain and can still be accessed without having to take out the sacks, but new items can not be stored anymore if you have more items stored than what the sacks in your inventory or [[Sack of Sacks]] allow for.
The capacity of a sack can be increased by applying [[Pocket Sack-in-a-Sack]] up to 3 times. Using a [[Pocket Sack-in-a-Sack]] for the first time on a sack will additionally increase it's [[Rarity]], although this is only cosmetic. Sack size can also be increased by up to 10% using the [[Hideonsack Shard]].
All items within sacks can be quickly sold using the quick sell option at the [[Bazaar]].
Sacks cannot be traded or auctioned.
## Tiers
There are four tiers/sizes of sacks in total. Each tier allows the player to store more items than the tier before it.
|Size|Amount of each item stored|Amount in stacks|
|-|-|-|
|Beginner|640|10|
|Small|2,240|35|
|Medium|6,720|105|
|Large|20,160|315|
Beginner sacks only contain vanilla items.
Some sacks, like [[Gemstone Sack|Large Gemstone Sack]] or [[Bronze Trophy Fishing Sack]] have unique amount of slots.
Holding multiple sacks of the same type adds their capacities.

## Bugs
- Sacks only combine their capacities if they match all items they can hold.
  - Notable sacks that do not add their capacities:
    - Beginner size sacks with the other sizes.
    - [[Nether Sack]] with [[Mining Sack]] ( [[Netherrack]], [[Nether Quartz]], [[Mycelium]], [[Red Sand]], [[Glowstone Dust]]), [[Agronomy Sack]] ( [[Nether Wart]]), and [[Combat Sack]] ( [[Blaze Rod]], [[Ghast Tear]], [[Magma Cream]])
    - [[Lava Fishing Sack]] with [[Mining Sack]] ( [[Coal]] and [[Netherrack]]), and [[Combat Sack]] ( [[Magma Cream]])
  - If one of the sacks that hold the resources has bigger storage, the smaller one can show that it's overfilled.
## Trivia
- If you [[Recombobulator 3000|recombobulate]] a sack, it will appear recombobulated but its rarity will not change.
  - Note: There is no benefit to recombobulating a sack, it's purely visual.
- You can hold up to 12,418,560 of each item in one sack if you were to have 80 sacks with 3 Pocket Sack-in-a-sacks used on each one of them.

Tooltip: Holds Vinyls dropped by  Pests!; Items you pickup go directly into your sacks.; Capacity: 20,160 of each item Multiple sacks sum their capacity!; Right Click to open!; EPIC SACK
The Vinyl Sack is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Sacks|Sack]] that stores 20,160 of [[Pest Vinyls]].
## Obtaining
A Vinyl Sack can be purchased from [[Pesthunter Phillip]] for 100 [[Pests (Currency)|Pests]].
## Usage
The Vinyl Sack can store [[Pest Vinyls]].

# Attack Speed
Aliases: Bonus Attack Speed
Categories: Pages with Missing Recipes, Player Statistics, Article stubs, Mechanics
[Attack Speed]
Type: Stat
Uses: Increases Attack Speed
Increasing: [[Reforging]], Certain Items, Pets, [[Accessory Power]], [[Crochet Tiger Plushie]] and more
[Values]
Max Value: 100
[Symbol]
Unicode Code Point: U+E001
Icon (The icon displayed in the Equipment Menu interface.): Golden Axe ()
Attack Speed is a Combat Stat that affects the speed of the [[Player]]'s attack rate by reducing the damage immunity frames of the entity hit.
## Mechanics
Attack Speed decreases the number of invulnerability ticks an enemy is granted upon hitting them, as well as the shot cooldown of [[Shortbows]].
The maximum Attack Speed is 100 , doubling the attack rate. The cap can be increased to 150 using the [[Thermodynamic Armor]]'s Full Set Bonus.
For melee hits, the invulnerability ticks are rounded to the nearest integer, resulting in the maximum effective Attack Speed being 82 . On the other hand, shot cooldown ticks for shortbows are always rounded up, meaning 100 is required to achieve maximum fire rate.
The formula for the invulnerability ticks of an enemy on hit is the following, with the brackets indicating rounding to the nearest integer: InvulnerabilityTicks=⌊101+AS100⌉
|Invulnerability Ticks|Attack Speed Lowest|Attack Speed Highest|
|-|-|-|
|10 (0.50s)|0|5|
|9 (0.45s)|6|17|
|8 (0.40s)|18|33|
|7 (0.35s)|34|53|
|6 (0.30s)|54|81|
|5 (0.25s)|82|122|
|4 (0.20s)|123|150|
The formula for the shot cooldown of most shortbows is the following, with the brackets indicating rounding up: ShotCooldownTicks=⌈101+AS100⌉
|Ticks per Shot|Attack Speed Lowest|Attack Speed Highest|Shots per Second|
|-|-|-|-|
|10 (0.50s)|0|11|2.00|
|9 (0.45s)|12|24|2.22|
|8 (0.40s)|25|42|2.50|
|7 (0.35s)|43|66|2.86|
|6 (0.30s)|67|99|3.33|
|5 (0.25s)|100|149|4.00|
|4 (0.20s)|150|150|5.00|
Note: Due to a vanilla Minecraft limitation, the maximum achievable fire rate while holding Use Item/Place Block (right click on default controls) is 4.00 shots per second regardless of the player's Attack Speed, unless it is bound to a keyboard key in Minecraft controls.
The formula for the shot cooldown of the [[Spirit Shortbow]] is the following, with the brackets indicating rounding up: ShotCooldownTicks=⌈151+AS100⌉
|Ticks per Shot|Attack Speed Lowest|Attack Speed Highest|Shots per Second|
|-|-|-|-|
|15 (0.75s)|0|7|1.33|
|14 (0.70s)|8|15|1.43|
|13 (0.65s)|16|24|1.54|
|12 (0.60s)|25|36|1.67|
|11 (0.55s)|37|49|1.82|
|10 (0.50s)|50|66|2.00|
|9 (0.45s)|67|87|2.22|
|8 (0.40s)|88|114|2.50|
|7 (0.35s)|115|149|2.86|
|6 (0.30s)|150|150|3.33|
The formula for the shot cooldown of the [[Spirit Shortbow]] is the following, with the brackets indicating rounding up: ShotCooldownTicks=⌈141+AS100⌉
|Ticks per Shot|Attack Speed Lowest|Attack Speed Highest|Shots per Second|
|-|-|-|-|
|14 (0.70s)|0|7|1.43|
|13 (0.65s)|8|16|1.54|
|12 (0.60s)|17|27|1.67|
|11 (0.55s)|28|39|1.82|
|10 (0.50s)|40|55|2.00|
|9 (0.45s)|56|74|2.22|
|8 (0.40s)|75|99|2.50|
|7 (0.35s)|100|133|2.86|
|6 (0.30s)|134|150|3.33|

## Hits Per Second
The maximum hits per second with [[Ferocity]] and Attack Speed can be calculated using this calculator:
calcpage  = Calculator:Hit Count
 template  = Template:Calculator/Hit Count
 form      = hitCountCalcForm
 calcname  = Hit Count Calculator
 result    = inner
 
 param     = basehitcount|Base Hit Amount|1|int
 param     = ferocity|Ferocity|0|int
 param     = attackspeed|Attack Speed|0|int
Loading Calculator...
If the calculator fails to load up, please report to an Administrator.
## Trivia
- The [[Zombie Soldier Cutlass]] currently gives +100 Attack Speed, the most out of any weapon.
  - The [[Livid Dagger]] used to give +100 Attack Speed, but it was lowered to +50 Attack Speed.
- When Attack Speed was implemented, a common misconception was that it affected [[Mining Speed]].
- The Unicode code point for Attack Speed is U+E001, meaning its icon could have been made first.
- [[Speed]], Attack Speed, [[Ferocity]], [[Swing Range]], [[Mining Spread]], [[Gemstone Spread]], [[Timber]], [[Fishing Speed]], [[Trophy Chance]], [[Treasure Chance]], [[Magic Find]] and [[Charm Chance]] are the only stats with a cap.
  - Of which [[Speed]], Attack Speed and [[Fishing Speed]] are the only ones whose cap can be changed.
## Notes
- Attack Speed works on arrows, decreasing the time before another arrow can hit the target. This only applies outside [[Dungeons]], because arrows do not grant invincibility frames in Dungeons.

# Crit Damage
Aliases: Critical Damage
Categories: Pages with Missing Recipes, Temp/Using Skill Aliases, Player Statistics, Article stubs, Combat, Crit Damage, Mechanics
[Crit Damage]
Type: Stat
Uses: Increases damage dealt by a critical hit.
Increasing: [[Armor]], [[Enchantments]], Reforges ([[Reforging]]), [[Pets]], [[Accessory Power]] and more
[Values]
Base Value: 50
Max Value: N/A
[Symbol]
Unicode Code Point: U+E007
Icon (The icon displayed in the Equipment Menu interface.): Player Skull ()
Crit Damage is one of the 17 core player [[Stats|statistics]]. It increases damage dealt upon landing a critical hit, which is determined by your [[Crit Chance]].
## Mechanics
When the player's [[Crit Chance]] activates, a critical hit is dealt. When a critical hit lands, the [[Damage Calculation|damage indicator]] displayed will change appearance from gray to red, orange, yellow, and white colors instead.
### Damage Calculation
[[Crit Chance]] Critical hits deal increased [[Damage Calculation|Final Damage]] based on the Crit Damage stat.
## Increasing Base Crit Damage
Players have a base Crit Damage of 50% by default. It can be increased by:
- Leveling [[Spider Slayer]] ( up to 18 crit damage)
- Leveling [[Wolf Slayer]] (up to 3 crit damage)
- Consuming [[Vial of Venom]] (up to 5 crit damage)

## Achieving highest possible Crit Damage outside Dungeons
Base: Base Crit Damage and bonus from leveling [[Slayer]] (50+18=68)
Accessories: [[Luxurious Spool|Silky]] reforge, Crit Damage [[Enrichments]], [[Accessories]] and [[Red Claw Artifact]] (30+944+5=979)
Armor: Full set of [[Necron's Armor|Necron Armor]] with the [[Reforging|Ancient]] reforge with [[Legion|Legion V]] ((30+50)*4=320)
Weapon: [[Reforging|Rapid]] [[Terminator]] with [[Overload|Overload V]] (250+5+90=345)
[[Ender Dragon Pet]] with [[Pet Items|Serrated Claws]] (50+25=75)
Temporary bonuses: [[Beacon]], [[Spirit Potion|Spirit]] and [[Critical Potion]] (40+40=80)
Combining all of that together (while having 20 players around): (68+979+320+345+75+80)*1.1*1.28=2642.82 Crit Damage
## Trivia
- It is possible to have negative Crit Damage, and it will decrease damage on a critical hit.
- The Crit Damage icon is identical to the particles that spawn when performing a critical hit in vanilla minecraft.

# 9f™ Network
Aliases: 9f Network, 9f™ Network Operator Chip, 9f™ Operator Chip, Relay
Categories: Pages with UIs, Quests
[9f™ Network]
9f™ Network Relay 9f™ Operator Chip [[9f™ Network]] Bomb
Tooltip: Allows you to do maintenance on relays for the Abiphone network.; Wear Fairy Armor to view relays.; Upgraded Relays: [#]\ 9; Bonus Health: +2 Health; Add [#] contacts to your Abiphone for Tia to offer the next tip.
The 9f™ Network is a [[Quests|Quest]] given by [[Tia the Fairy]] which spans the [[SkyBlock]] world, and requires the [[Player]] to repair 9f™ Network Relays by selecting correct sounds.
## Quest
After adding [[Tia the Fairy]] on the player's [[Abiphones|Abiphone]], a 9f™ Operator Chip will be added into it. If [[Tia the Fairy|Tia's]] [[Abiphones|contact]] is removed, the Chip - alongside any rewards granted by it - will be removed as well and only granted again once the contact is readded.
The [[Player]] can then call [[Tia the Fairy|Tia]] for a hint on where a 9f™ Network Relay might be found. In order to be able to see the Relays, the [[Fairy Armor]] needs to be worn. There are a total of 9 Network Relays, each of which can be repaired by playing a minigame. The more of them has the [[Player]] repaired, the greater the reward granted by the 9f™ Operator Chip, as shown in a table below:
9f™ Network:
|Relay|Contacts; Needed|Coordinates|Location|Cumulative Rewards|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|1|1|143, 108, 93 ([[Coordinates]])|[[Wilderness]]|+2 [[Health]]|
|2|5|-246, 123, 55 ([[Coordinates]])|[[Ruins]]|+4 [[Health]]|
|3|9|129, 232, 201 ([[Coordinates]])|[[Dwarven Mines]]|+6 [[Health]]|
|4|13|-558, 163, -286 ([[Coordinates]])|[[The End]]|+8 [[Health]]; +1 bonus contact|
|5|17|-374, 207, -798 ([[Coordinates]])|[[Blazing Volcano]]|+10 [[Health]]; +1 bonus contact|
|6|21|-68, 157, -878 ([[Coordinates]])|[[Cathedral]]|+12 [[Health]]; +1 bonus contact|
|7|25|93, 86, 187 ([[Coordinates]])|[[Wilderness]]|+14 [[Health]]; +1 bonus contact|
|8|29|0, 146, -75 ([[Coordinates]])|[[Dungeon Hub]]|+16 [[Health]]; +2 bonus contact|
|9|33|-23, 86, -12 ([[Coordinates]])|[[Village]]|+18 [[Health]]; +2 bonus contact|
After completing all relays, [[Fred]] will no longer have connection issues when calling him through the Abiphone.
## Gameplay
The 9f™ Network is a minigame heavily reliant on audio, thus using headphones is recommended. To first find the relays on your own requires the [[Fairy Armor]] set to be worn. When near the area specified by [[Tia the Fairy|Tia]], a pulsating sound can be heard that gets faster and higher pitched when approached. Clicking the relay will initiate the minigame.
The player is given 4 columns with 4 tiles to choose from each column, and one column is available at a time starting from the left. To start, the player should right click the four available tiles first to hear the audio that plays. These audio tracks are mainly vanilla sounds for example fireworks or mob groaning, with variations in pitch. The goal is to click each one of the 4 tiles from the lowest pitch to the highest. When the player clicks the tiles in the wrong order, the tiles will be disabled and cannot be clicked anywhere between 3 seconds to instantaneously being re-enabled, after which the column will be reset but not the whole board, giving some lenience for some mistakes even in the final relays.
Starting from the third relay, bombs will occasionally appear between the columns after finishing the first column. The bombs must be disarmed by clicking them within three seconds, otherwise they will explode and immediately fail the minigame. They appear more frequently the higher the relay, but fortunately retains the three second countdown.
The first relay will give the player 60 seconds to solve all 4 columns. The following relays will shorten the timer by 3 to 4 seconds with the ninth relay giving you only 30 seconds to finish it.
## Trivia
- Although the 9f™ Operator Chip's tooltip implies that [[Abiphones|contacts]] requirement is only required to receive a "tip" from [[Tia the Fairy|Tia]], in fact, the next Network Relay will not appear at all until the [[Player]] reaches required number of contacts and calls [[Tia the Fairy|Tia]].
- The 9f™ Operator Chip has the same texture as the [[Punchcard Artifact]].
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Oct 13, 2022|AHN 0.16|Added 9f™ Network.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Oct 19, 2022|0.16|Added 9f™ Network.|

Abiphones, short for Ability Phones, are items that allow the player to call [[NPC|NPCs]] remotely without having to interact with them physically.
## Obtaining
[[Alda]] sells the Abiphone Basic at [[Abiphones & Co.]] in the [[Hub Island|Hub]]. All other Abiphones can be purchased from [[Udel]] or [[Nuvian]] in the [[Crimson Isle]].

## Usage
### Contacts
Certain [[NPC|NPCs]] have contacts, and the player can obtain them by completing quests given by the [[NPC]]. To obtain Abiphone dialogue, the player must right-click the desired [[NPC]] with an Abiphone in hand. Each unique contact grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+10 SkyBlock XP]]. This can only be completed once per contact.
- Abiphone Contacts cannot be obtained from [[Garden Visitors|Visitors]] on the [[The Garden|Garden]] and instead must be obtained normally.
