### Contact Slots
An extra +3 contact slots can be obtained from using a [[Abiphone Contacts Trio]]. This can be used up to 25 times, granting a total of +75 contact slots, and is added on top of the current model's contact limit.
An extra +1 contact slot can be achieved from the [[Abicases|Sumsung© G3 Abicase]], or an extra +2 contact slots from the [[Abicases|Sumsung© GG Abicase]].
Up to +2 extra contact slots can be achieved from completing [[9f™ Network]] relays.
### Extra Features
Some Abiphones that have a high enough model have extra features. Tic Tac Toe and Snake are games that can be played through an interface, whilst the Contact Directory allows players to view the full list of contacts, see which ones they are missing and organize the list alphabetically.
### Overview
The Contact Directory is a feature available for all Abiphones that allows players to browse the complete contact list, re-arrange it, and discover info about each individual contact.
Models:
- [[Abiphones|Abiphone Basic]]
- [[Abiphones|Abiphone X Red]]
- [[Abiphones|Abiphone X Blue]]
- [[Abiphones|Abiphone XI Yellow]]
- [[Abiphones|Abiphone XI Green]]
- [[Abiphones|Abiphone XII Amber]]
- [[Abiphones|Abiphone XII Amethyst]]
- [[Abiphones|Abiphone XIII Jade]]
- [[Abiphones|Abiphone XIII Sapphire]]
- [[Abiphones|Abiphone XIV Orange]]
- [[Abiphones|Abiphone XIV Purple]]
- [[Abiphones|Abiphone XIV Black]]
- [[Abiphones|Abiphone Flip: Dragon]]
- [[Abiphones|Abiphone Flip: Nucleus]]
- [[Abiphones|Abiphone Flip: Volcano]]
- [[Abiphones|Abiphone Flip+: Flower]]
- [[Abiphones|Abiphone Flip+: Clockwork]]
- [[Abiphones|Abiphone Flip+: Lunar]]
- [[Abiphones|AⒷiphone]]
### Usage
The Contact Directory can be sorted into different categories, to ease navigation:
- All
- Missing
- Completed Quest but not Active
- A-Z
- Z-A
On top of categorisation, the Contact Directory a quick overview of the NPC, shows where they are usually located, the requirement to obtain their contact, and text displaying the difficulty for obtaining it. The difficulties that can be seen are:
- Easy
- Medium
- Hard
### Overview
Do Not Disturb Mode is a feature available for Abiphone XII and above models that allows players to silence individual NPCs' incoming calls.
Models:
- [[Abiphones|Abiphone XII Amber]]
- [[Abiphones|Abiphone XII Amethyst]]
- [[Abiphones|Abiphone XIII Jade]]
- [[Abiphones|Abiphone XIII Sapphire]]
- [[Abiphones|Abiphone XIV Orange]]
- [[Abiphones|Abiphone XIV Purple]]
- [[Abiphones|Abiphone XIV Black]]
- [[Abiphones|Abiphone Flip: Dragon]]
- [[Abiphones|Abiphone Flip: Nucleus]]
- [[Abiphones|Abiphone Flip: Volcano]]
- [[Abiphones|AⒷiphone]]
### Overview
Snake is a game, present in high enough Abiphone XIII and above models, that allows players to play the classic game.
Models:
- [[Abiphones|Abiphone XIII Jade]]
- [[Abiphones|Abiphone XIII Sapphire]]
- [[Abiphones|Abiphone XIV Orange]]
- [[Abiphones|Abiphone XIV Purple]]
- [[Abiphones|Abiphone XIV Black]]
- [[Abiphones|Abiphone Flip: Dragon]]
- [[Abiphones|Abiphone Flip: Nucleus]]
- [[Abiphones|Abiphone Flip: Volcano]]
- [[Abiphones|AⒷiphone]]
### Gameplay
Snake games start with a snake head in the middle of the menu. The player can navigate the snake, causing it to move in all directions except diagonally. The aim is to make the snake consume the apples that appear in random slots. Each time an apple is eaten, the snake grows a longer body, each piece symbolising one point. The game ends when the snake crashes into either itself or the edge of the menu.
The player's top score is kept track of, and can be viewed in the Abiphone's main menu.
### Overview
Tic Tac Toe is a game, present in Abiphone XIV and above models, that allows players to play the classic game against an unbeatable AI.
Models:
- [[Abiphones|Abiphone XIV Orange]]
- [[Abiphones|Abiphone XIV Purple]]
- [[Abiphones|Abiphone XIV Black]]
- [[Abiphones|Abiphone Flip: Dragon]]
- [[Abiphones|Abiphone Flip: Nucleus]]
- [[Abiphones|Abiphone Flip: Volcano]]
- [[Abiphones|AⒷiphone]]
### Gameplay
Tic Tac Toe games start in a 3 by 3 grid, with the AI being on the Red team, and the player on the Green team. When either player selects a block, it will change from [[Wool|White Wool]] to wool of their team's color. The game ends when either the player or AI scores three blocks in a row, column or diagonal, or both teams draw.
Previous scores are kept track of, and can be viewed in the Abiphone's main menu.
### Overview
Ringtones is a feature available for Abiphone Flip and above models that allows players to set the tone that plays when calling or being called by an NPC.
Models:
- [[Abiphones|Abiphone Flip: Dragon]]
- [[Abiphones|Abiphone Flip: Nucleus]]
- [[Abiphones|Abiphone Flip: Volcano]]
### Overview
Speed Dial is a feature that allows the player to instantly call any NPC upon left-clicking their Abiphone, skipping the process of selecting the [[NPC]] in the menu. It is only available on Abiphone Flip+ models.
To put an [[NPC]] on Speed Dial, the player must right-click the contact and set their contact to Speed Dial. Only one [[NPC]] can be set to Speed Dial. The following interface uses [[Rusty]] and [[Maddox the Slayer]] as examples.
## Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Invalid [[NPC]]|[✆] You cannot add this NPC as a contact...|
|Invalid [[NPC]]|[✆] This NPC doesn't own an Abiphone...|
|Invalid [[NPC]]|[✆] This NPC is scared of the advent of cyborgs in SkyBlock and therefore refuses to own an Abiphone…|
|Invalid [[NPC]]|[✆] The NPC slips you a piece of map with numbers written on it. Sadly they wrote it too small…|
|Invalid [[NPC]]|[✆] This NPC has an allergy to electromagnetism. It's a rare condition that isn't catalogued yet by the medicine community...|
|Invalid [[NPC]]|[✆] This NPC's Abiphone plan doesn't include unlimited calls and texts, so they prefer keeping their contact to themselves...|
|Invalid [[NPC]]|[✆] The NPC is nonplussed by the technology you present them...|
|Invalid [[NPC]]|[✆] This NPC had their Abiphone stolen by a Dante goon and the warranty has expired…|
|Already on contact list|[✆] This NPC is already in your contacts list!|
## Tips
- Due to the delay by ringing, [[Queen Mismyla]]'s contact can be used for easier item switching to a mining tool with [[Mining Wisdom]], giving more [[Mining]] experience when claiming a reward.
- While calling [[Maddox the Slayer]] might seem slower than using [[Maddox Batphone]], it can be sped up by using the /call command, the Speed Dial feature and/or [[Abicases|Rezar® Abicase]].
## Trivia
- Currently, there are 85 contacts that can be added, and the maximum contact slots is 92.
- [[Kaus]] and [[Rollim]] will continue to call the player even if they have completed their training.
- The message [✆] This NPC has an allergy to electromagnetism. It's a rare condition that isn't catalogued yet by the medicine community... is a reference to the show Better Call Saul.
  - Specifically, it references a condition that Chuck McGill, the protagonist's brother, claims to have.
- Currently, there is no purpose of Maximum Discs, as [[Music Discs]] cannot be played with an Abiphone.
  - [[Udel]] mentioned the absence of the function in Udel's Abiphone Keynote: "It would be better if they were playable..."
- According to [[Tomioka]]'s dialogue, their great-grandfather invented the Abiphone.
- It used to be possible to put a [[Power Scrolls|Power Scroll]] on an Abiphone. Upon using the Abiphone, the effect is given upon opening the menu, with the same cooldown applying to the effect as if it were any other right-click item.
  - This could be used to apply its effects, such as using it before a fight for added [[Strength]] or [[True Defense]] when facing tougher mobs or [[Mining Speed]] when mining.
  - This has since been patched and is no longer possible. however, Abiphones that already had a [[Power Scrolls|Power Scroll]] applied still keep it.
- [[Alixer]] is an Abiphone contact exclusive for [[Bingo|Ⓑ Bingo]] profiles, which does not grant [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock XP]]. It is unknown whether it would grant [[Accessory Power]], as [[Abicases]] are unobtainable on Bingo.
- [[Udel]] used to sell Abiphones at 98.5% of their base prices, while [[Nuvian]] sold them at 101.5% of their base prices.
- Prior to 0.23.4, lower-tiered Abiphones could be traded in to reduce the [[Coins]] cost on the next tier.
- Despite some NPCs being located in [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]], there is no reception inside dungeons, so even NPCs like [[Duncan]] cannot be reached from within them.

# Accessory Bag
Aliases: Colossal Accessory Bag, Giant Accessory Bag, Greater Accessory Bag, Humongous Accessory Bag, Large Accessory Bag, Massive Accessory Bag, Medium Accessory Bag, Preposterous Accessory Bag, Small Accessory Bag, Talisman Bag, Titanic Accessory Bag
Categories: Pages with UIs, Upgrades, Redstone Dust Collection, Accessories, SkyBlock Menu
[Accessory Bag]
Type: Storage upgrade
Appearance: Pouch with an accessory inside
Collection: Redstone Dust II (2) ([[Redstone Dust]])
Usage: A special bag which can hold Talismans, Rings, Artifacts and Orbs within it. All will still work while in this bag!
The Accessory Bag is a [[Bags|Bag]] that allows players to store [[Accessories]]. While in the Accessory Bag, stored Accessories will grant [[Accessory Power]], which grants [[Maxwell|Tuning Points]] that can be used to optimize players' [[Stats]] to their liking.
## Obtaining
Accessory Bag can be accessed through the [[Bags]] section of the [[SkyBlock Menu]] and can be increased through various ways. 9 Accessory Bag slots are unlocked for everyone.
### Redstone Collection
The Accessory Bag increases in size through the [[Redstone Dust]] collection, the collection gives a maximum of 48 slots.
|Slots|Collection| [[Redstone Dust]] Required|
|-|-|-|
|6|Redstone II (2) ([[Redstone Dust]])|250|
|12|Redstone III (3) ([[Redstone Dust]])|750|
|18|Redstone IV (4) ([[Redstone Dust]])|1,500|
|24|Redstone V (5) ([[Redstone Dust]])|2,500|
|30|Redstone VI (6) ([[Redstone Dust]])|5,000|
|36|Redstone VII (7) ([[Redstone Dust]])|25,000|
|42|Redstone VIII (8) ([[Redstone Dust]])|100,000|
|48|Redstone IX (9) ([[Redstone Dust]])|250,000|
### Redstone Miner
At the [[Pigmen's Den]], the [[Redstone Miner]] requests some [[Redstone Dust]] in exchange for a total of 4 slots.
### Community Center
In the [[Community Center]], the Accessory Bag can be further expanded through [[Account & Profile Upgrades|Account Upgrades]], up to 12 slots in the accessory bag.
|Tier|Time|Slots|
|-|-|-|
|I|4 Days|2|
|II|6 Days|4|
|III|8 Days|6|
|IV|10 Days|8|
|V|14 Days|10|
|VI|18 Days|12|
### Jacobus
[[Jacobus]] sells 2 slots per purchase in exchange for [[Coins]], up to 198 slots in the accessory bag.
### Attributes
The [[Attributes|Accessory Size]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which is obtained from syphoning the [[Hideonring Shard]], adds 1 slot per level, up to 10 slots in the accessory bag.
## Usage
The Accessory Bag can hold any [[Accessories]] and [[Hatcessories]] within it.
Within the Accessory Bag, the effects of Accessories will apply to the player, including its [[Accessory Power]].
## Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Player did not select Accessory Bag power|Woah! You didn't select an Accessory Bag power! Visit Maxwell the Thaumaturgist in the Hub to fix that ASAP!|
|Player has blacklisted items in their Accessory Bag|You had # blacklisted items in your Accessory Bag, they have been deleted! Sorry!|
## Trivia
- The Accessory Bag was called the Talisman Bag prior to Update 0.13, which introduced [[Accessory Power]]. It is still called talisman_bag in the [[SkyBlock API]].
- Unlocking the Greater Accessory Bag upgrade grants the [[Achievements|Accessories Galore]] achievement.
- Certain counters, such as the [[Beastmaster Crest]] counter, do not show properly within the Accessory Bag; requirements to use certain accessories such as [[Slayer]] accessories will also show as if the player has not reached them. However, their stats and effects will still work.
- It costs 1,761,500,000 [[Coins]] to reach the maximum upgrade at [[Jacobus]].

# Achievements
Aliases: Achievement, Rough Deal
Categories: Pages with UIs, Mechanics
[Achievements]
Achievements are in-game goals that the player can accomplish and award achievement points for unlocking cosmetic features on the Hypixel server game lobbies.
There are two types of Achievements: Tiered and Challenge. The former requires the player to repeat the same process until they complete each tier of the Achievement. The latter can only be completed once, and require the player to complete a specific action.
## Interface
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!

# Andrea
Aliases: Safari Milestones
Categories: Pages with UIs, Non-player characters
[Andrea]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Gives out the [[Safari Belt]] and talks about [[Critter Safari]] Milestones.
Location: [[Critter Safari Entrance]]
Coordinates: -711.5 108 178.5
Andrea is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Critter Safari Entrance]] on the [[Torrhus Canyon]]. They give out the [[Safari Belt]] and talk about [[Critter Safari]] Milestones.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Andrea: Have you got your very own Safari Belt yet? All of our customers get one! Select an option: [No]|
|Selecting [No]|[NPC] Andrea: Well, please take this spare one!|
|Selecting claiming the [[Safari Belt]]|[NPC] Andrea: The Safari Belt is the only item you can take with you into the Critter Safari. [NPC] Andrea: You'll find it already equipped once you enter - for your convenience. [NPC] Andrea: Whenever you reach a new Safari Milestone, your Safari Belt will upgrade either its stats or its rarity. [NPC] Andrea: It's kind of like David's Cloak, if you think about it. [NPC] Andrea: You can progress through each biome's milestones by catching Critters while in that biome. Simple, right? [NPC] Andrea: Anyways, if you ever want to check your progress towards your next Safari Milestone, just come and chat with me!|

# Archer
Categories: Pages with UIs, Dungeons, Classes
The Archer Dungeon Orb
Archer is a [[Dungeons|Dungeon]] [[Classes|class]] that specializes in dealing extreme [[Damage]] through ranged attacks using [[Bows]]. It shares the area-clearing ability of the [[Mage]] and the tank-slaying ability of the [[Berserk|Berserker]], but, in return, is extremely fragile. This class relies heavily on [[Crit Damage]] and [[Strength]], much like the Berserk class. It is the best class for damage per second (DPS).
## Class Passives
- Doubleshoot: 50% Chance to shoot a second arrow.
- Bone Plating: Reduces your next incoming hit by X [[Damage]] (scales with Archer level and [[Crit Damage]]) Cooldown: 250 seconds
- Bouncy Arrows: X% chance for your arrow to bounce to an additional target. Cooldown: 15 seconds
## Dungeon Orb Abilities
- Explosive Shot: Shoots 3 arrows that explode on impact, dealing the same [[Damage]] as your highest bow hit in the last minute in a 4 block radius. Can also explode things similarly to [[Superboom TNT]].
- Rapid Fire: Shoots 5 arrows per second for 4 seconds! Arrows deal 75.0% of your highest bow hit in the last minute.
## Ghost Abilities
- Healing Bow: Shoot a teammate to heal them for 10% of their max [[Health]] and grant them +50 [[Strength]] for 10 seconds. Cooldown: 10 seconds Note: does knockback to teammates.
- Stun Bow: Temporarily stuns any non-boss Dungeon monster for a short period of time when shot. Cooldown: 5 seconds
## Leveling Rewards
You can level up the Archer class while playing as an Archer or if you have a teammate that is an Archer, both of which provide Archer XP as a team bonus.
For every Archer level up you get:
- +1% Extra Arrow Chance
- +1.6% Arrow [[Damage]]
- +2 Bouncy Arrow Chance
For every 5 Archer levels you additionally get:
- -2 Explosive Shot Cooldown
For every 10 Archer levels you additionally get:
- +1 Rapid Fire Duration
At Archer level 50, you receive the following total buffs:
- 101% Extra Arrow Chance
- +230% Arrow Damage
- 100% Chance for Arrows to bounce of to another target
- -20s Explosive Shot cooldown, lowering the cooldown to 20s
- +5s Rapid Fire Duration, for a total of 8s
## Milestones
The Archer class can reach its milestones by dealing bow damage in the Dungeon.
## Trivia
- The [[Rarity|Bone Plating]] passive might be a reference to a League of Legends rune, as both function very similarly and have the same name.

# Attribute Fusion
Aliases: Chameleon Fusion, Fusing, Fusion, Fusion Box, Fusion Machine, Fusions, ID Fusion, Shard Fusion, Special Fusion
Categories: Pages with UIs
[Attribute Fusion]
Attribute Fusion is the process of combining different [[Attributes|Attribute Shards]] to obtain new Shards. This process can be done in the [[Fusion House]] in [[Tangleburg]], or remotely with [[Kysha]]'s [[Abiphones|Abiphone Contact]].
## Usage
Interacting with the Fusion Machine opens a menu which lets you select the Shards you wish to fuse. Once two Shards are selected, a list of Shards that you can obtain upon fusing your selection is displayed.
The number of Attribute Shards needed for fusion depends on the initial input:
- [[Chameleon Shard|Chameleon]] requires 1 Shard
- Reptile, Amphibian and Elemental Shards (e.g. [[Gecko Shard|Gecko]], [[Eel Shard|Eel]], [[Mossybit Shard|Mossybit]], [[Bolt Shard|Bolt]], etc.) require 2 Shards
- In all other cases, 5 Shards are required
The number of Attribute Shards outputted during fusion depends on the type of fusion.
- ID Fusion and Chameleon Fusion yield 1 Shard
- Special Fusion yields 2 Shards
- Using any Reptile-type Attribute Shard, including [[Chameleon Shard|Chameleon Shards]], has a chance to activate the [[Attributes|Pure Reptile]] Attribute which grants a 2%-20% chance to double output shards during a fusion.
## Fusion Recipes
Inputting 2 Attribute Shards together will output a maximum of 3 new Shards: only one output can be selected with each fusion. There are 3 'types' of Fusion: each type will yield different outputs, and they are outlined below. Fusion recipes can never output one of their inputs.
## Fusion Types
### ID Fusion
ID Fusion recipes function sequentially. Each Shard has a pre-defined "ID result". It typically follows a pattern of having the same category and rarity of the Shard. Then, an algorithm is used to determine the results from ID Fusion.
Pseudocode:
if shard1's category = shard2's category then
   if shard1's rarity = shard2's rarity then
       if shard2's id result = shard1 then
           id result = shard1's id result
       else
           id result = shard2's id result
   else
       id result = higher rarity shard's id result
else
    id results = shard1's id result, shard2's id result
### Special Fusion
Special Fusion recipes consist of pre-defined recipes that output a specific Attribute Shard. Special Fusion recipes generally follow a specific thematic pattern depending on the family and rarity of the input Shards.
### Chameleon Fusion
Chameleon Fusion is a special type of Fusion that only works with a [[Chameleon Shard]]. Fusing with a [[Chameleon Shard]] is guaranteed to output the next 3 eligible IDs of the Shard of the other input, even if any of the given Shards outputted are not regularly obtainable from fusion (e.g. [[Gecko Shard]]) with the exceptions of these shards:
- [[Bitbug Shard]]
- [[Doomspiral Shard]]
- [[Macaw Shard]]
- [[Shinyfish Shard]]
- [[Galaxy Fish Shard]]
- [[Molthorn Shard]]
- [[Starborn Shard]]
- [[Primordial Shard]]
- [[Rainbug Shard]]
- [[Timestalk Clone Shard]]
- [[Wumpa Shard]]
Only one [[Chameleon Shard]] is required for the input, and only one Shard will be outputted.
If no correct shard is found, the first shards of the next rarity will be outputted (for example: R63 + [[Chameleon Shard]] will result in R64, E1 and E2). Legendary rarity shards are the exception; if no correct shard is found, the output slot will be empty.
## Experience Rewards
Upon a successful fusion, some XP will be rewarded dependent on the rarity of the resultant fusion.
|Rarity|XP gained|
|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|+18.75 Hunting XP ([[Hunting]])|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|+37.5 Hunting XP ([[Hunting]])|
|RARE ([[Rarity]])|+75 Hunting XP ([[Hunting]])|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|+125 Hunting XP ([[Hunting]])|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|+250 Hunting XP ([[Hunting]])|
## Trivia
- Shards typically follow a pattern in category per ID line: the first Shard will always be Forest-type, followed by Water and Combat.
## Bugs
Contrary to its description, the Smart Fusion mechanic is currently bugged to only consider input shards eligible for fusion if the player has not maxed out the corresponding Attributes yet.
